# The Orientation Boundary: claim index

[Repository](README.md) · [Claims](#claims) · [Modules](#modules) · [Source files](#source-files) · [Additional results](#additional-results)

This index links the paper's results to their Lean declarations and source files. Numbers follow the paper; labels also identify supporting results in the Lean development. Names beneath a Lean namespace use that namespace as their prefix.

The paper's Concrete Witness System (CWS) is named `KO7` in Lean.

Each entry gives the conditions and the recorded Lean status. `DEFINITION` marks a definition. `PROVEN / EXTERNAL` includes external tool evidence.

<a id="claim-to-anchor"></a>
## Claims

### Def. 3.1

`def:schema` · **DEFINITION**

A step-duplicating schema is a carrier with base, successor, wrap, and recursor containing the duplicating rule.

**Lean result.** The structure packages those four operations on a type, which every schema barrier quantifies over.

**Source.** [StepDuplicatingSchema.lean][m1]

<details>
<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

- `OperatorKO7.StepDuplicating.StepDuplicatingSchema`

</details>

### Def. 3.2

`def:compositional` · **DEFINITION**

Tier 1 additive measures use constructor weights with wrap weight at least one; Tier 2 uses combiners with wrap-subterm axioms and successor transparency at base.

**Lean result.** The two structures are the additive and abstract compositional axiom systems used by the schema barrier theorems.

**Conditions.** Tier 1: positive wrap weight. Tier 2: wrap-subterm axioms.

**Source.** [StepDuplicatingSchema.lean][m1]

<details>
<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

Lean namespace: `OperatorKO7.StepDuplicating.StepDuplicatingSchema`

- `CompositionalMeasure`

</details>

### Rem. 3.3

`rem:classic-instance` · **PROVEN**

The textbook rule f(x,s(y)) to g(x,f(x,y)) is a schema instance, so every schema barrier specializes to it with its source hypotheses.

**Lean result.** The full-stack structure records every unconditional branch and every conditional branch with its necessary premise; the affine, restricted-quadratic, max-plus, pair-matrix, mixed-matrix, and constructor-law fields are unconditional.

**Conditions.** Conditional fields retain only their schema-level dominance, positivity, attainment, transparency, or scalarization premises; the dummy base parameter is ignored by the recursor projection.

**Source.** [TextbookDupInstance.lean][m2], [TextbookDupInstance_FullStack.lean][m3]

</details>

### Def. 3.4

`def:trace` · **DEFINITION**

The canonical trace is the wrapper chain around the recursor as the counter counts down from k to the base rule.

**Lean result.** The canonical-trace definition is that state, with one-step duplicating and base rules on a base-duplicating system.

**Conditions.** One-step variants need no context closure; multi-step variants use WrapContextClosed.

**Source.** [SchemaCanonicalTrace.lean][m4]

### Def. 3.5

`def:trace-coords` · **DEFINITION**

Along each live canonical-trace stage the counter, payload-multiplicity, and wrapper-multiplicity coordinates are k minus i, i+1, and i.

**Lean result.** The three Nat functions compute those coordinates.

**Conditions.** Live stages satisfy $0\le i\le k$.

**Source.** [SchemaCanonicalTrace.lean][m4]

### Prop. 3.6

`prop:per-step-exchange` · **PROVEN**

Each canonical step decreases the counter by one and increases payload multiplicity by one.

**Lean result.** For every i less than k, those two equalities hold of the counter and payload coordinates.

**Conditions.** i < k.

**Source.** [SchemaCanonicalTrace.lean][m4]

<details>
<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

- `OperatorKO7.StepDuplicating.StepDuplicatingSchema.BaseDuplicatingSystem.per_step_exchange`

</details>

### Prop. 3.7

`prop:offset-conservation` · **PROVEN**

Payload multiplicity equals wrapper multiplicity plus one at every live stage.

**Lean result.** The coordinate identity holds, and the formal trace theorem proves that payload occurrences equal emitted wrapper constructors plus one for every $i\le k$.

**Conditions.** Live stages satisfy $0\le i\le k$.

**Source.** [SchemaCanonicalTrace.lean][m4], [TraceInvariants.lean][m5]

</details>

### Def. 3.8

`def:wrapper-cell` · **DEFINITION**

A wrapper cell has weight wrap-size plus payload-size, and the wrapper stack is the length-i list of identical payload copies.

**Lean result.** The cell-weight and stack definitions realize those objects; the length-offset theorem is the identity with payload multiplicity.

**Source.** [SchemaOffsetAndWrapper.lean][m6]

### Prop. 3.9

`prop:counter-retained` · **PROVEN**

The counter is the unique coordinate that is gauge-invariant and strictly descending along the canonical trace.

**Lean result.** The package proves strict counter descent, payload and wrap ascent, and gauge invariance of the constant payload tuple.

**Conditions.** i < k for the strict-descent clause.

**Source.** [SchemaOffsetAndWrapper.lean][m6]

### Def. 4.1

`def:cost-gain` · **DEFINITION**

Wrapper cost and counter gain are the integer differences of the two rule sides from the shared recursive term.

**Lean result.** The two functions compute those differences in the integers for every schema and every natural-valued function.

**Source.** [CouplingTheorem.lean][m7]

### Thm. 4.2

`thm:orientation-criterion` · **PROVEN**

A measure orients a duplicating instance if and only if its wrapper cost lies below its counter gain.

**Lean result.** The pointwise and the uniform equivalence for every schema and every natural-valued function.

**Source.** [CouplingTheorem.lean][m7]

<details>
<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

Lean namespace: `OperatorKO7.Methods.OrientationClosure.CouplingTheorem`

- `orients_all_iff_wrapperCost_lt_counterGain`

</details>

### Thm. 4.3

`thm:coupling-inequality` · **PROVEN**

Retention with orientation forces the gain above the retained payload plus the cost; a bounded gain with unbounded values excludes orientation; the polynomial interpretation attains the margin.

**Lean result.** The inequality at every triple, the barrier under a uniform gain bound and unbounded values, and gain $s+2$ against retention cost one for the example.

**Conditions.** Retention $c_w+M(x)+M(y)\le M(\mathrm{wrap}(x,y))$; for the barrier a gain bound and unbounded values.

**Source.** [CouplingTheorem.lean][m7]

<details>
<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

Lean namespace: `OperatorKO7.Methods.OrientationClosure.CouplingTheorem`

- `retained_wrapper_forces_payload_coupled_gain`
- `no_orientation_of_retention_bounded_gain_unbounded_payload`
- `main_polynomial_coupling_tight`

</details>

### Thm. 4.4

`thm:direct-duplication-law` · **PROVEN**

A measure that keeps both wrapper arguments at a fixed cost and gains at most a fixed amount per counter step fails uniform strict orientation of the duplicating step; so does every relation whose strict comparison forces strict decrease of the measure, on every system containing the step.

**Lean result.** The structure packages the two clauses; the three theorems prove the pointwise, tracked-relation, and system-level forms from a positive seed pumped by wrapper retention, with no range hypothesis.

**Conditions.** Wrapper retention at cost c_w; counter gain at most g.

**Source.** [NonlinearUnconstrainedExactLaw.lean][m8], [CouplingTheorem.lean][m7]

### Thm. 4.6

`thm:four-cells` · **PROVEN**

Every measure lies in a growth cell; the barrier cell excludes strict and nonstrict orientation; each other cell holds an orienter and a non-orienter.

**Lean result.** Coverage by the two dichotomies, exclusion at the chosen pair, and the six-example catalog on closed free terms.

**Conditions.** Items (i) and (ii): every schema and every natural-valued function. Item (iii): the free schema over the empty variable type.

**Source.** [CellClassification.lean][m9], [CellClassificationFamilies.lean][m10]

### Thm. 5.12

`thm:premise-necessity` · **PROVEN**

For each of the twenty-seven consumed premises of the twelve direct families, maximum depth, and head precedence (fourteen families), a measure on the free schema satisfies the other premises, violates that premise, and orients the duplicating rule, so the barrier with that premise removed is false and the barrier with it holds; the three families with an empty premise list have empty slot types; the theorems also hold with the unused premises removed.

**Lean result.** The countermodel catalog over every slot, the restore-and-refute pair, the slot count and the family list facts, the empty slot types, and the theorems with the unused wrapper, subterm and offset premises removed.

**Conditions.** Free schema for the countermodels; the theorems with unused premises removed hold on every step-duplicating schema.

**Source.** [DirectFamilyHypothesisNecessity.lean][m11]

### Sec (method rows)

`sec:premise-use` · **PROVEN**

Every method row of the universe carries one necessity statement of one of four classes, stated on the row's own laws and acceptance, and its proof; the class counts are computed; arctic and tropical interpretations reject the nonduplicating successor rule in contexts.

**Lean result.** The capstone over the twelve families, both cell hypotheses and the seventy-six rows; the row evidence; the counts; the list equality with the universe; the masking theorems.

**Conditions.** The declared method universe; each statement's shape is recorded in its module docstring.

### Cor. 4.8

`cor:families-barrier-cell` · **PROVEN**

Each direct family lies in the barrier cell at the base pair or fails at an explicit triple, so its barrier is an instance of the cell theorem.

**Lean result.** Class lemmas from the family equations and the self-nested wrapper chain, each proved without the barrier theorem of its family, and the grammar form on the counter and payload carrier.

**Conditions.** The family equations with their wrapper conditions; the dominance premises of the cross-term, multilinear, and polynomial families; the tracked scalar of the vector families.

### Thm. 4.14

`thm:context-lift` · **PROVEN**

Contextual orienters are root orienters; the strict context laws lift root orientation to the context closure; the minimum wrapper separates the two.

**Lean result.** The identity-context restriction, the barrier cell at the context level, the lift with termination under a well-founded relation, and the control pair.

**Conditions.** Item (ii): strict context laws, and a well-founded relation for termination.

**Source.** [ContextLiftMetatheorem.lean][m15]

### Thm. 5.1

`thm:schema-barrier` · **PROVEN**

No additive or transparent-compositional whole-term measure orients the duplicating step.

**Lean result.** For every additive measure with positive wrap weight, and every compositional measure with wrap-subterm axioms and successor transparency at base, the duplicating step is not strictly decreasing.

**Conditions.** Tier 1: positive wrap weight. Tier 2: wrap-subterm axioms and transparency at base.

**Source.** [StepDuplicatingSchema.lean][m1]

<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

Lean namespace: `OperatorKO7.StepDuplicating.StepDuplicatingSchema`

- `no_compositional_orients_dup_step_transparent_succ`

</details>

### Cor

`cor:ordered-carrier-schema-barrier` · **PROVEN**

The schema barrier extends to ordered additive carriers and arbitrary preorders, with a countermodel to deleting attainment.

**Lean result.** Additive orientation fails under attained successor weight, in particular under nonnegative base weight; transparent composition fails in every preorder; the negative-integer model orients when attainment fails.

**Conditions.** Additive branch: partially ordered commutative additive monoid and attained successor weight. Compositional branch: preorder, right-wrapper subterm, transparency at base.

**Source.** [SchemaBarrier_OrderedCarrier.lean][m16]

<summary>Lean declarations</summary>

</details>

### Thm. 5.2

`thm:affine-barrier` · **PROVEN**

No constructor-local affine measure orients the duplicating step.

**Lean result.** The first two theorems block affine orientation from the class axioms alone; the three pumped forms are the triple-returning extractors under unbounded range, a successor pump, or a wrap/base pump.

**Conditions.** Wrapper coefficients at least one.

**Source.** [BarrierPumpDischarge_Schema.lean][m17], [StepDuplicatingSchema.lean][m1]

### Thm. 5.3

`thm:polynomial-general-barrier` · **PROVEN**

No degree-bounded polynomial table measure with eventual fixed-base target dominance orients the duplicating step, and every orienter in the family violates that dominance.

**Conditions.** Finite polynomial table; eventual fixed-base target dominance; the unbounded forms add a growth premise.

### Full arctic-class split following Cor. 5.11

`cor:arctic-barrier` · **PROVEN**

The unrestricted one-coordinate arctic family has a universal necessary condition and a counterexample to its removal.

**Lean result.** Finite tracked wrapper diagonals are blocked, so strict orientation forces nonfiniteness; a bottom-diagonal model orients; a constant finite-diagonal model does not.

**Conditions.** The split is unconditional over the stated one-coordinate class; the recorded elaboration, build, and paired axiom checks passed.

**Source.** [EscapeTrichotomyExtended.lean][m27]

### Thm

`thm:matrix2-lex-barrier` · **PROVEN**

No finite tracked-primary lexicographic affine measure whose primary coordinate takes a positive value orients the duplicating step.

**Lean result.** The first two theorems replace the pump by one positive value of the primary coordinate; the pumped forms remain as triple-returning extractors.

**Conditions.** Finite tracked-primary lexicographic order, one positive value of the primary coordinate. That premise is necessary (`OperatorKO7.StepDuplicating.StepDuplicatingSchema.zeroAffineMeasure_nonstrict_orients`).

### Sec. 12 (the boundary as an object)

`sec:formalization` · **PROVEN**

The Orientation Boundary is a set of grammar expressions with a semantic and a syntactic description and the two sets are equal; the counter projection lies inside, the payload projection and every constant outside; the growth cells form a four-element type, every measure lies in one cell at each pair and in no second cell, and the barrier cell excludes orientation.

**Lean result.** Set equality by extensionality from the capstone biconditional; membership facts from the definitions; coverage from four_cell_coverage; uniqueness by instantiating the bounds; the classical choice of the cell is a function by uniqueness.

**Conditions.** None beyond the capstone and cell theorems.

**Source.** [OrientationBoundaryObject.lean][m97]

### Thm. 4.11

`thm:free-profile-attainment` · **PROVEN**

Numeric profiles are attained on free root steps; root height is the least natural ranking and is not profile-factored.

**Lean result.** Proves attainment, orientation equivalence, pointwise minimality, and the equal-profile unequal-height pair; the AttainedPairs copies state attainment and the orientation equivalence on the four-constructor syntax with variables used by the boundary theorem.

**Conditions.** Closed seven-constructor syntax for the height result; the four-constructor syntax with variables for the attainment half; the two defined observations; root rewriting, not contextual execution length.

**Source.** [FreeRecursorWitnessOrder.lean][m98], [AttainedPairs.lean][m99]

### Prop

`prop:safestep-mw-complexity` · **PROVEN**

Every SafeStepCtx chain has length at most the Cichon value of the contextual notation built from ctxFuel; the root note is not a direct contextual descent.

**Lean result.** The length bound is the conservative lift; the obstruction theorem shows the root note can increase on a contextual step.

**Conditions.** Conservative ctxFuel-to-lex3Note lift; a theorem obstructs contextual reuse of the root note.

**Source.** [SafeStep_Complexity_MW_Ctx.lean][m138]

**[DirectMeasureGrammarClosure.lean][m95]**

Scalar grammar-closure barrier over the (counter, payload-mass) carrier. Every orienting grammar measure is payload-blind and counter-strict, and those two conditions suffice. Under `CounterAdmissible`, orientation is equivalent to payload-blindness. The counter projection supplies an inhabited case; the constant-zero expression pins the omitted-premise failure (`PayloadBlind`, `CounterStrict`, `CounterAdmissible`, `eval_section_const_or_unbounded`, `orients_implies_payload_blind`, `orients_implies_counterStrict`, `payloadBlind_and_counterStrict_implies_orients`, `orients_iff_payloadBlind_and_counterStrict`, `counterAdmissible_orients_iff_payloadBlind`, `counter_orients_via_exact_boundary`, `const_zero_not_orients`), baseline $\{\mathrm{propext},\ \mathrm{Quot.sound}\}$

Syntax-free carrier-level form of the grammar closure.
